# L7-figure-census — writer claims

One pid, one writer. Append-only: add a row when you start a paper, and do not edit or
delete anyone else's row. A row claims the paper while it is being worked; a closed paper
also has a `records/<pid>.json` on disk, which is the authority on whether it is done.

Queue order is `queue.txt`. Ranges below are a no-collision agreement, not a rule about
which papers may be recorded — anyone may claim the next free paper in their own range.

Nothing in the paper is three-dimensional and no exhibit carries a colourbar, so every value is read off a position against a printed tick; keys are inside the frame six times, outside right once (the four-frame map row) and absent eleven times; panel grammar is sixteen single frames, two four-panel plates (the map row and Figure 14's 2x2 matrix, whose top-left cell is left empty), and six two-frame pairs captioned once and measured twice. Families: node-link-network 2 (Figure 1 flat with a viridis colour bar, Figure 3 the same layout in 3-D with node size carrying the Fiedler cut), composite-panel 1 (Figure 2, histogram plus sorted curve, shared x scale), flowchart-or-schematic 1 (Figure 4, a timing diagram with the blocked event in red), bar 1 (Figure 5, 9 density groups x 5 response delays, flat at 8x magnification), scatter 1 (Figure 6, one open circle per room, two blue reference rules at 0.1 and 0.9), map-or-plan-view 1 (Figure 7, flood-fill density on the gallery plan, keyed only by the words 'Mona Lisa' printed in the art), heatmap-or-contour 1 (Figure 8, 2x2 barcode strips, each panel with its OWN colour bar on a different scale - 0-8000 top row, 0-6000 below - so the quad compares by pattern, not by colour), line 1 (Figure 9, five area series with crossing translucent fills). NINE FIGURES, EIGHT FAMILY LABELS, no family used twice except node-link-network. Depth exactly once and only to make a cut visible. Legends 2 of 9, both outside right and both on the Excel-style result charts; 6 of 9 print numeric ticks; ZERO data labels anywhere. Colour is spent on the exception in three figures (red bar among blue, magenta cut line, the one saturated yellow room). Captions: 'Figure N:' with the COLON ON 9 OF 9 (the lane's opposite case to #176), 13-22 words, full stop on only 5 of 9, two of them carrying a second sentence that explains the encoding or the notation (Figures 1, 4), 9 of 9 below the art. Six of nine figures are named in prose, three (1, 6, 7) never; read-backs are quantified and confined to the results half (three for Figure 5, two for Figure 9). NO DEFICIT: every printed caption has a measured region - the paper has nothing to miss because it prints nothing tabular. LAYER DEFECTS: the caption detector truncates Figure 7 at 'highest around the' because the art's own annotation interrupts the run; the footer counts 19 pages for a 20-page file) |
